# Produced by the C++ drivers' own output, via the ignored capture test of the same file (it writes both files
# of this golden without their headers; the headers are written by hand):
#   SAI_MACSEC_CAPTURE_DIR=<dir> cargo test -p sai-macsec --test differential -- --ignored capture_golden_from_the_cpp
# inside rust/sai's package environment (nix/package.nix; SAI_OPENBCM_SRC = ai_re/nix/packages/sai-teralynx/frontend/src,
# SAI_INC = the sairedis inc above). Inputs: 595 scripts and the 9 tables they run on, all in
# golden/differential-inputs.txt; fixed seeds: 16 backbones (4 table variants x 4 ciphers), 272 failing
# sequences (2 directions x 8 faults x 17 positions), 7 named scripts, 300 random scripts = seeds
# 20261126 .. 20261425 of tests/common/seeded.rs (proptest 1.12.0 ChaCha; "rnd-<seed>"). The stored inputs are
# the truth: 'the_golden_inputs_are_the_fixed_corpus' checks that the generators of the test still make them.
# Outputs are normalised as the test does (the longest-run-time column of a status port line is replaced by "?"),
# then digested: <lines>:<FNV-1a 64 of the normalised text, with each line's "\n">. Full texts are kept for the
# named cases and one backbone (bb-g1p1a0-c3), as "== text <id> <plain|fixed>" sections, lines prefixed ">"; where plain equals fixed only "fixed" is kept.
# rust=differs (named-d1): the Rust differs from the C++ there by design (sai-macsec D1: a flow moved to another
# port); only the C++'s own output is kept, and 'the_golden_keeps_the_documented_differences' asserts the lines.
# NOT to be regenerated from the Rust output (the C++ goes away at H5; regenerating from Rust would make the test
# compare Rust with itself). A deliberate behaviour change edits the affected lines by hand, with a
# "# changed <date>: <reason>" line here.
# Proof that the golden is what the live C++ printed (at capture, 2026-11-26): 'the_golden_equals_the_live_cpp'
# (595 scripts: plain + fixed digests equal, the 8 kept texts equal, "listed" equal), next to the 14 live tests.
# Negative controls: 'the_golden_notices_a_change'.
